# THE EXAM DOCKET — Deuteronomy 15:1-23 (the release — at the end of every seven years you shall make a release, every creditor shall release what he lent to his neighbor, he shall not exact it of his neighbor and his brother because the LORD's release has been proclaimed, of the foreigner you may exact; the needy and the blessing — there shall be no needy among you, for the LORD will surely bless you in the land if only you diligently hearken, you shall lend to many nations and not borrow, rule over many nations and they shall not rule over you; the hand opened — if there is among you a needy man of your brothers you shall not harden your heart nor shut your hand, open, you shall open your hand and lend him sufficient for his need, beware lest there be a base thought in your heart saying the seventh year, the year of release, draws near, give, you shall give and your heart shall not be grieved, the needy shall never cease out of the land; the Hebrew slave — if your brother, a Hebrew man or a Hebrew woman, is sold to you he shall serve six years and in the seventh you shall send him free, you shall not send him away empty, furnish, you shall furnish him from your flock, your threshing floor and your winepress, remember that you were a slave in Egypt and the LORD redeemed you; the awl and the double hire — if he says I will not go out from you because he loves you and your house, take the awl and put it through his ear and into the door and he shall be your servant for ever, likewise to your maidservant, it shall not seem hard when you send him free for double the hire of a hireling he served you six years; the firstling — every firstling male born of your herd and of your flock you shall sanctify to the LORD, you shall do no work with the firstling of your ox nor shear the firstling of your flock, before the LORD you shall eat it year by year in the place He will choose, you and your household; the blemish and the blood — if it has a blemish, lame or blind, any ill blemish, you shall not sacrifice it, within your gates you shall eat it, the unclean and the clean alike, as the gazelle and as the hart, only its blood you shall not eat, on the earth you shall pour it as water) — THE DEUTERONOMY WALK sitting 13b, THE DOCKET IN THREE RUNS (2026-09-23; DEUTERONOMY_WALK.md "Sitting 13b — THE COMPILE OF CHAPTER 15 … THE DESIGN", THE ORDER; its "THE DOCKET — AS RUN (D1)", "(D2a)" and "(D2b)" paragraphs)

Gamliel sent to the sages: a blemish; the kid's ear doubled — one bone, a blemish; not one bone, none; R. Chananya b. Gamliel: the kid's tail like a pig's, or without three vertebrae — a blemish. The 'other blemishes' beyond the list (Sifra, Emor, Chapter 3 1: 'blemish... blemish' amplifies) decided case by case; recorded rulings.

Yehuda's arm carried as a rejected reading.
- Mishnah Bekhorot 6:11 [TOPIC — CREDITED: priesthood_topic_docket_2026-09-06.md] — LAW. Antigonus: the hock in the middle of the thigh. ON THESE BLEMISHES THE FIRSTLING IS SLAUGHTERED AND THE DISQUALIFIED CONSECRATED ARE REDEEMED: the table's closing rule — the same list serves two institutions (Deut 15:21's firstling, Lev 27's redemption of the blemished consecrated — cold_run_temurah), read off 22:20-25's altar bar.
- Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12 [TOPIC — CREDITED: priesthood_topic_docket_2026-09-06.md] — LAW. CREDITED (priesthood_topic_docket_2026-09-06.md; carried) — THESE ARE NOT SLAUGHTERED ON, neither in the Temple nor in the province: the cataract and the water NOT permanent, the inner teeth notched (not uprooted), the garav, the wart, the chazazit, the OLD, the SICK, the FOUL, the one sinned with, the one that killed a man (by one witness or the owner), the tumtum and the androgynos; R. Yishmael: no blemish greater; the sages: it is no firstling — shorn and worked. THE PASSING-BLEMISH CLASS: barred from the altar (Sifra, Emor, Section 7 3: 'ALL in which is a blemish' — a passing blemish included) yet no license to slaughter — the two verdicts on one animal; garav and yalefet (22:22) read as the CURABLE forms here against the dry scab and the Egyptian lichen (Section 3 15); the sinned-with and the killer = Temurah 6:1's class; the doubtful sex.

## Bekhorot
- Bekhorot 25a:1 [TOPIC] — LAW. (the sugya's tail) permitted to pluck the hair to make the slaughter proper: F6 — the shearing's edge (Mishnah Bekhorot 3:3 — the neck's hair plucked, not shorn; 24b:5 in D1).
- Bekhorot 25a:2 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav's consistency — the barrel's cloth stopper on a Festival (the unintentional squeezing) against his leave to clear with a cleaver.
- Bekhorot 25a:3 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. R. Shimon concedes 'cut off its head and will it not die' — the inevitable result.
- Bekhorot 25a:4 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav rules as R. Yehuda (an unintentional act forbidden), Shmuel as R. Shimon — the traditions' names.
- Bekhorot 25a:5 [TOPIC] — LAW. Rav: PLUCKING IS NOT SHEARING — the cleaver's clearing an uprooting in an unusual manner, not forbidden by the Torah: F6 — the shearing's definition (a DATA row: what the ban names).
- Bekhorot 25a:6 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the bird's feather on Shabbat — plucking the wing is shearing, snipping is cutting, pulling the threads is smoothing; a wing's plucking its normal removal, unlike hair.
- Bekhorot 25a:7 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav as R. Yosei ben HaMeshullam (plucking not shearing) — and does R. Yosei ben HaMeshullam hold an unintentional act forbidden?
- Bekhorot 25a:8 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the red heifer's two black-tipped hairs cut with scissors (R. Yosei ben HaMeshullam) — no intent to shear: the consecrated animal's shearing ban's edge.
- Bekhorot 25a:10 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the red heifer consecrated for the Temple's maintenance — R. Elazar's rabbinic ban on shearing and working such; the heifer uncommon, the decree not made.
- Bekhorot 25a:11 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. desacralize, shear and reconsecrate? too costly; Shmuel's peruta after the fact, not from the outset.

- Bekhorot 25a:13 [TOPIC] — LAW. the Mishnah's clearing 'with a cleaver from here and from there' — Rav Ashi in Reish Lakish's name: by hand yes, with a tool no (it looks like shearing); read 'with his hands for a cleaver': F6 — the shearing's edge (a DATA row).
- Bekhorot 25a:14 [TOPIC] — LAW. plucking to show a Sage the blemish's place — permitted from the outset: R. Yosei ben HaMeshullam's wool tangled in the ear plucked and shown: F6 — the shearing's edge for the examination (F7's expert); a DATA row.
- Bekhorot 25a:15 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Mari's inference from 'and likewise' — not the moving of the hair.

## Tosefta Sheviit
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:1 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the court's agents at the town gates take the seventh-year produce and give back three meals' worth, the rest to the storehouse: OUTSIDE the release — the seventh-year produce's storehouse (yovel.sabbatical by CALL); a DATA row.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:2 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the figs, the grapes and the olives harvested by the court's hired workers into the storehouse, distributed on Sabbath eve by household: OUTSIDE — the produce's law; a DATA row.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:3 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. after the removal the poor eat, not the rich (R. Yehuda); R. Yose: both; R. Shimon: the rich from the storehouse: OUTSIDE the release — the removal's persons (the produce's law); the arms; a DATA row.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:4 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the removal's declaration — 'our brothers, whoever needs, come and take' — then eaten till it ends: OUTSIDE; a DATA row.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:5 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. the shop's debt — released (R. Yehuda), not (the Sages); Rabbi: R. Yehuda's words for a money debt, the Sages' for a produce debt: F1 — the_release_object's SHOP-CREDIT arm given a split (Mishnah Sheviit 10:1's dispute); the exam's arm.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:6 [TOPIC] — LAW. the marriage contract — compromised AND extended as credit: released; one without the other: not: F1 — the_release_object's marriage-contract edge (a debt turned into a loan); the exam's row.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:7 [TOPIC] — LAW. the pledge-loan not released though the loan exceeds the pledge; a note with a property lien not released: F1 — the pledge's exception (113:2; Mishnah 10:2) and the LIEN (Gittin 37a:10-11's arm — part C); the exam's row.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:9 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. R. Yehuda: the prosbol (the court's writ) signed by the judges inside and the witnesses outside; the Sages: a court's act needs no confirmation: F1 — the writ's signatures (Mishnah 10:4's 'the judges or the witnesses'): the arms; the exam's arm.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:10 [TOPIC] — LAW. five debtors on one note — a prosbol on whoever has land; R. Shimon ben Gamliel: one's land for all; the perforated pot as land (R. Shimon: even unperforated); R. Chutzpit: the wife's land for her husband: F1 — the LAND condition's cases (Gittin 37a:3-5's Hillel); the exam's rows.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:11 [TOPIC] — LAW. the prosbol written on the eve of the seventh year's New Year; one written on the eve of the following New Year collects for ever, even torn; R. Shimon ben Gamliel: loans after the writ not released: F1 — the writ's DATE against the_release_date (the writ before the release's moment); the exam's rows.
- Tosefta Sheviit 8:12 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the robber and the usurer who repent and return — not accepted; the Sages displeased with one who accepts: OUTSIDE — the interest's law (yovel.interest by CALL); a DATA row.

## Tosefta Kiddushin
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:1 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the wife acquired three ways (money, a writ, intercourse) and acquires herself two: OUTSIDE — the family's law (Mishnah Kiddushin 1:1's chapter read for 1:2-3's slave); a DATA row.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:2 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. 'the land full of lust' (Leviticus 19:29) — the single man's intercourse (R. Eliezer), the bastards (R. Eliezer ben Yaakov), the withheld rains (R. Yehuda): OUTSIDE; a DATA row.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:3 [TOPIC] — LAW. THE DEDUCTION OF PRICE defined — the slave redeems himself within the six years by the money and the years remaining, THE SLAVE WITH THE UPPER HAND; the presumption of land (a lock, a fence, an opening) and of Canaanite slaves (the shoe, the vessels, the lift): F4 — the exits' table's DEDUCTION exit (Mishnah Kiddushin 1:2; mishpatim_3.maidservant(the_two_exits) by CALL; the advantage Arakhin 30a:19's I2); the presumptions OUTSIDE; the exam's row.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:4 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. the Hebrew slave acquires himself by the major limbs (the export's Exodus 21:26), by money through others and a writ by himself (R. Meir — 'the left hand to the right'); the Sages: either, with others' money on condition; R. Shimon ben Elazar: the writ by others only: F4 — the exits' MEANS: the arms (mishpatim_2.slave_maimed by CALL for the limbs); the exam's arm.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:5 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the ship by drawing (R. Natan: and by a writ); drawing and handing over defined; where movables are acquired: OUTSIDE — the acquisition's law; a DATA row.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:6 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. land for land, movables for movables, each for the other — the liability at the taking: OUTSIDE; a DATA row.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:7 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the Temple's acquisition by the treasurer's money and by speech, the layman's by drawing: OUTSIDE; a DATA row.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:8 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the time-bound commandments; the son's duties to the father; the father's to the son — to circumcise, TO REDEEM HIM (the firstborn's five selas), to teach Torah and a trade, to marry him: OUTSIDE the chapter (ordinances.gifts(five_sela) by CALL for the firstborn son); a DATA row.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:9 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rabban Gamliel's parables of the trade — the fenced vineyard, the married woman: OUTSIDE; a DATA row.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:10 [TOPIC] — LAW. R. Elazar son of R. Shimon: the commandments not binding before the entry apply only in the Land — EXCEPT the remission of loans, the redemption of sales and THE SENDING OF THE HEBREW SLAVE: in the Land and outside it: F1 — the_release_territory's IN-AND-OUT arm at the Tosefta (Kiddushin 38b:1's twin — the slave's sending the third, 15:12's law with it); the exam's row.
- Tosefta Kiddushin 1:11 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. one commandment tips the scale; the righteous who rebels at the end; Scripture, Mishnah and a trade — the threefold cord: OUTSIDE; a DATA row.

## Tosefta Bekhorot
- Tosefta Bekhorot 1:1 [TOPIC] — LAW. the donkey's fetus bought from a gentile, sold to him, partnered, received — exempt: 'in Israel' (Numbers 3:13), not in others; priests and Levites liable for the clean firstborn and exempt from the unclean by 'of man and of beast' (Numbers 8:17); the two redemptions to priests alone: F6 — consecrated_firstborn's PERSONS (the gentile's share exempts — Bekhorot 9b:13's partnership; ordinances.firstling(donkey_by_call) by CALL); the exam's rows.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 1:2 [TOPIC] — LAW. only the donkey among the unclean beasts; the dog's price alone barred (23:19 ahead); R. Meir's praise of the donkey's commandment; R. Yosei HaGelili: 'a firstborn ox, sheep, goat' (Numbers 18:17) — the mother and the offspring of one kind, or some of the mother's signs: F6 — the firstling's KIND from Numbers 18:17 (korach.the_gifts by CALL); the exam's rows.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 1:3 [TOPIC] — LAW. the clean's unclean-looking offspring permitted, liable with some signs; the unclean's clean-looking forbidden — what emerges from the unclean is unclean, from the clean clean: F6 — the offspring rule (chapter 14's F2 by CALL to food_tithe.the_beasts); the exam's row.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 1:4 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the unclean fish that swallowed a clean one and the reverse: OUTSIDE — the food law's; a DATA row.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 1:5 [TOPIC — CREDITED: deu_14_reeh_exam_2026-09-21.md] — DISPUTE. CREDITED (deu_14_reeh_exam_2026-09-21.md; carried) — bees' honey permitted (ingested, not secreted), the zizin's forbidden; R. Shimon: 'camel, camel' (Leviticus 11:4; 14:7) includes the camel born of a cow, permitted if like its mother; the Sages: what emerges from the unclean is unclean and from the clean clean; the gestation table; dolphins; fish and fowl eaten by tradition, the hunter believed: F2 — the offspring rule's two arms at the Tosefta's seat; THE BIRD BY TRADITION the_birds_signs' second arm (F3); the gestation table DATA rows; the exam's arm.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 1:6 [TOPIC] — LAW. the cow's lamb-like offspring — not for the donkey's redemption; 'seh' (a lamb or a kid) everywhere includes sheep and goats, adult and young, male and female, whole and blemished; R. Elazar's hybrids; THE DONKEY'S THIRTY DAYS — redeemed or its neck broken (R. Yosei son of R. Yehuda: not less than a shekel; the hatchet from behind, buried, no benefit); 'ONE IS BARRED FROM SHEARING AND FROM WORKING IT IN ITS LIFETIME, R. SHIMON PERMITS; a human firstborn permitted in everything'; two males from a donkey — two lambs: F6 — firstling_work_and_shearing_barred's TOSEFTA SEAT with R. Shimon's arm (the row's object the firstborn donkey — the arm's reach a DATA row) and the donkey's law (ordinances.firstling — thirty days, redeem_or_break — by CALL); the exam's rows.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 2:1 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. the cow's fetus bought from a gentile, sold, partnered, received — exempt; R. Yehuda: the receiver is credited its full value and gives half to the priest; the Sages: the gentile's finger in the middle exempts: F6 — consecrated_firstborn's PARTNERSHIP edge, two arms; the exam's arm.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 2:2 [TOPIC] — LAW. ownerless, consecrated and the heirless convert's livestock exempt; the property-tax livestock liable: F6 — the firstling's OWNER condition; the exam's rows.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 2:3 [TOPIC] — LAW. the consecrated whose permanent blemish preceded the consecration, redeemed — liable to the firstborn and the gifts, their offspring common, NO LASHES for shearing and working them; whose consecration preceded the blemish — exempt from the firstborn and the gifts, offspring consecrated, LASHES for shearing and working before and after redemption: F6 — the shearing-and-work BLOCK's reach over the blemished consecrated (Bekhorot 15b's table — chapter 12's credited rows; temurah by CALL); the exam's rows.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 2:4 [TOPIC] — LAW. the iron flock (the gentile's guaranteed flock) — the offspring exempt, the offspring's offspring liable; the mothers dead — one generation on; R. Shimon ben Gamliel: all exempt for ever: F6 — the partnership's generations; the exam's arm.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 2:5 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. the ewe's goat-like offspring exempt unless some signs; two males' heads at once — R. Yosei HaGelili both the priest's ('the males', Exodus 13:12), the Sages one; R. Tarfon the better, R. Akiva the less; THE DOUBT TABLE WHOLE — the two ewes, the one that had given birth, R. Yosei's gifts rule, R. Meir's 'give it to me or its gifts': F6 — the doubt table at the Tosefta (Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6-8's twin, its choices reversed against 2:6's 'they compromise'); the exam's arms.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 2:6 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. the animal bought from a gentile — the goat's offspring within a year, the ewe's within two, the cow's within three the priest's for certain (R. Yishmael; the donkey like the cow, R. Yosei son of R. Yehuda four); R. Yehoshua: 'you have erred' — the SIGNS of a birth (the discharge, the afterbirth, the woman's sac); R. Akiva: known, unknown, doubtful; R. Eliezer ben Yaakov: the blood mass buried, the next exempt: F6 — suspect_firstborn's AGE TABLE (Mishnah Bekhorot 3:1's ground; korach.the_gifts(suspect_firstborn) by CALL): the arms; the exam's arms.
- Tosefta Bekhorot 2:7 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. the nursing animal bought from a gentile — the next a doubtful firstborn; R. Shimon ben Gamliel: the presumption holds (the flock at night, the gentile shepherd); THE WOOL of an unblemished firstborn set in a window — Akavya permits, the Sages forbid (R. Yehuda / R. Yosei on the case): F6 — the presumption's arm and the wool's status (Mishnah Bekhorot 3:4's twin); the exam's arms.

## The finds (this docket's crowns — the three runs)
- THE TWO RELEASES AT THREE BAVLI SEATS AND THE DECREE ARM (Gittin 36a:14, Kiddushin 38b:3, Moed Katan 2b:17 — Rabbi: "this is the manner of the release: he shall release" (15:2) speaks the land's release and the money's; when the jubilee is not in force the money's release stands by the Sages' decree in remembrance, Gittin 36a:13-36b:1, and the debtor told to sit still, or the court's power over property — 36b:3-4); THE TERRITORY'S OUTSIDE ARM FROM THE VERSE'S OWN CLAUSE (Kiddushin 38b:1, 38b:4 — "because the LORD's release has been proclaimed": in any case; Tosefta Kiddushin 1:10 the twin with the slave's sending as the third exception).
- HILLEL'S PROSBOL (the court's writ) WHOLE AT ITS FOLIO: the reason the verse's own warning (Gittin 36a:11 — 15:9), THE TEXT (Mishnah Sheviit 10:4; Gittin 36a:12), the ground (36a:13 — rabbinic against rabbinic), the land condition and its minimum, its substitutes and the perforated pot (Mishnah 10:6; Gittin 37a:3-7; Tosefta Sheviit 8:10), the writ by speech (37a:6), the date (Tosefta 8:11), the orphans' exemption (37a:2), THE PRESUMPTION "I had one and lost it" (37b:5-8 — a dispute), the scope for all generations (36b:5-10 — Shmuel's "I will nullify it", Rav Nachman's "I will uphold it"); the writ's signatures a dispute (Tosefta 8:9).
- THE MANNER "THE WORD OF THE RELEASE" AT FIVE SEATS (Mishnah Sheviit 10:8 read whole; Gittin 37b:1-2 — the creditor may lift his eyes until "nevertheless"; Shabbat 148b:12 with the edge of a loan made after the year) AND THE OATH RELEASED BY THE SAME WORD (Shevuot 49a:2 — Mishnah Shevuot 7:8; Tosefta Sheviit 8:8 with R. Shimon's arm).
- THE RELEASE'S OBJECT AT THE ANSWER SHEET AND ITS EDGES (Mishnah Sheviit 10:1-2 — the shop's credit and the wage unless made loans, the fines and the court's obligations, the pledge-loan and the notes handed to the court; Tosefta 8:5-7 the shop split by Rabbi, the marriage contract, the lien; THE LIEN'S TWO ARMS Gittin 37a:9-11; THE TEN-YEAR LOAN'S TWO VERSIONS Makkot 3a:15 / 3b:4; THE STIPULATION AGAINST THE RELEASE VOID IN ITS FORM, VALID AS THE DEBTOR'S WAIVER Makkot 3b:7-10; what cannot yet be exacted is not released — Bava Batra 145b:2, Gittin 30a:16; the thirty days from 15:9's doubled name Makkot 3b:11-14).
- THE COUNT RENEWED AT EZRA'S RETURN AND THE JUBILEE'S CONDITION (Arakhin 32b:10 — as at Joshua's arrival they counted sabbaticals and jubilees; 30:5's "your fathers possessed" 32b:11; THE JUBILEE NULLIFIED WHEN REUBEN, GAD AND HALF MANASSEH WERE EXILED — "to all its inhabitants", in their arrangement, 32b:15-17; THE FIFTIETH COUNTED TO ORDER THE SABBATICALS with the jubilee's laws not in force 32b:18; the fiftieth's arithmetic two arms 33a:1, Rosh Hashanah 9a:1), THE JUBILEE'S ONSET at the New Year with the slaves crowned till Yom Kippur (Rosh Hashanah 8b:8-12; Arakhin 28b:7 — the sabbatical at its END), the pierced slave's jubilee at Jeremiah's seat (Arakhin 33a:2-4 — two persons in 34:14).
- THE DOUBLED VERBS UNCONDITIONAL (Bava Metzia 31b:7-12 — "open, you shall open" to another city's poor, "give, you shall give" a small gift, "furnish, you shall furnish" though the house was not blessed, "lend, you shall lend" the one with means who will not use them; R. Elazar ben Azarya and R. Shimon: "the Torah speaks in the language of men" — the dissenting arms; Ketubot 67b:11 the same); 15:4's "no needy among you" A PRECEDENCE RULE for one's own property (Bava Metzia 30b:1, 33a:10; Sanhedrin 64b:15); 15:4 and 15:11 TWO LIVES (Taanit 21a:2-4) and Shmuel's unchanged world (Berakhot 34b:20; Shabbat 63a:10).
- THE MEASURE OF NEED AND THE LADDER (Ketubot 67b:2-3 — the house, the bed and the table, the wife by "for him"; Hillel's horse and the servant; the litra 67b:4; the two persons who refuse — the loan turned gift and the gift collected from the estate, 67b:8-12; the secret givers 67b:12-18; Bava Metzia 71a:3-4 — the ladder of Exodus 22:24 and the Jew before the gentile even at a loss).
- THE HEBREW SLAVE'S PERSONS AND HIS BOARD (Bava Metzia 71a:8-16 — the convert acquires as a gentile, THE WOMAN MAY NOT ACQUIRE A HEBREW SLAVE; THE PIERCED SERVES NO HEIR, 71a:14; "with you" — with you in food, drink and bedding, Arakhin 30b:2, Bava Kamma 87b:6, Ketubot 43a:11, 58b:7; the deduction defined with the slave's upper hand, Tosefta Kiddushin 1:3, Arakhin 30a:19's I2 on 15:18; the slave's institution tied to the jubilee by I2 "well"/"well", Arakhin 29a:18).
- THE AWL'S TOOLS AT FOUR SEATS AND THE UPPER EAR BY THE PRIEST'S BLEMISH (Bekhorot 37b:10-11, 51a:15, Shevuot 4b:11 — "and you shall take" anything by hand / Rabbi: metal; Bekhorot 37b:12-14 — the lobe (Yudan) against the cartilage (the Rabbis: a priest-slave is not pierced lest he be blemished), and the lentil for the slaughter against any hole for the altar).
- THE FIRSTLING SANCTIFIED BY WORD THOUGH HOLY FROM BIRTH (Nazir 4b:11; Arakhin 29a:20 — the two verses at the answer sheet; Bekhorot 19a:4 — 15:19's "males" the detail of Exodus 13:2; 41b:8, 42a:11 the sex's edge; the caesarean by I2 "birth"/"birth" Niddah 40a:14, Mishnah Bekhorot 2:9, Tosefta Bekhorot 3:2; the year passed no bar Temurah 21b:9), THE DOUBT TABLE (Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6-8; Tosefta Bekhorot 2:5-7; the age table 2:6) and THE PERSONS (Tosefta Bekhorot 1:1, 2:1-4 — the gentile's finger).
- THE WORK AND THE SHEARING (Bekhorot 9b:13 — "your ox … your flock" the partnership's arm; 24b:5 and Mishnah 3:3 — the neck's hair plucked, not shorn; 41b:2 the sanction gated on certainty; Chullin 135a:9, 135b:13, 136b:18, 137a:4 — the ban borrowed by the first shearing and read over both kinds; Temurah 11b:15; Tosefta Bekhorot 1:6 with R. Shimon's arm, 2:3 the blemished consecrated's lashes).
- THE BLEMISH CLASS BY GENERAL-PARTICULAR-GENERAL (Bekhorot 37a:16-19 — "any blemish", "lameness or blindness", "any ill blemish": EXPOSED AND NOT REGENERATING, I6 where the Sifrei's 126:1 read I8; the hidden out by "ill", the temporary out by reasoning and by the particulars' own choice, 37a:20-37b:5; the measures 37b:7-8; MISHNAH BEKHOROT 6:1 whole; the intended blemish barred, Mishnah 5:3), THE TESTIMONY AND THE EXPERT (35b:1-36b:15 — priests suspected, Israelites not; hearsay valid; the priest's own word; the ruling per R. Shimon ben Gamliel — the son and the daughter, not the wife; the three laymen where there is no expert, 36b:16-37a:3; the unexamined sale's sanction 37a:6-12; 15:22's "it" a limit, Yevamot 73b:15).
- THE THREE CASES OF THE HEBREW SLAVE AT THEIR SUGYA (Kiddushin 14b:7-15b:4): the analogy 'hired worker' / 'hired worker' (I2 — Leviticus 25:40 with 15:18) joins the self-seller to the court's sale; the baraita's four differences (14b:9-11 — the term six or more, the awl, the severance, the Canaanite maidservant) against R. Elazar's 'no difference'; the teacher who rejects the analogy is Rabbi (15a:23-15b:4 — the gentile's slave redeemed 'by any of these' relatives, never by six years); THE GENTILE'S SLAVE'S REDEEMERS (15b:5-14 — R. Yosei HaGelili by the verse PRECEDING (E28), R. Akiva by the following, the Rabbis every redemption to freedom; the dispute a matter of reason 15b:10-11).
- THE SEVERANCE GIFT'S PERSONS, MEASURE AND FEATURE: 'to him' — not the self-seller (15a:6), not his creditor (Tutai 16b:16; 15a:7 — not his heirs refused, a hired worker's wage passes); the sendings extended by 'you shall not send' and 'when you send' (16b:19), 'from you' excluding the fugitive and the one who deducts with R. Meir's arm (16b:20, 16b:17); THE MEASURE THREE ARMS WITH THEIR GROUNDS (17a:5 — five per kind by 'empty' / 'empty' with the firstborn 17a:7-8; thirty by 'giving' / 'giving' with the gored slave 17a:10; fifty by the valuations and 'poverty' / 'poverty' 17a:13-14; the selection rule 'grasped too much, grasped nothing' 17a:12; R. Meir's total not less 17a:6); THE FEATURE the category of blessing — money out (R. Shimon) or mules out (R. Eliezer ben Yaakov), 17a:16-17b:1; THE DOUBLED VERB 'grant, you shall grant' in any case (17b:2) against R. Elazar ben Azarya's plain reading — 'the Torah speaks in the language of men' (17b:3); the maidservant's gift to her father while he lives (16b:11-13), her brothers excluded (16b:14).
- THE EXITS' TABLES AT THE ANSWER SHEET AND ITS GEMARA: Mishnah Kiddushin 1:2 whole (14b:2); the modes' grounds — money from the gentile's sale and the maidservant's redemption (14b:3-8), the document from 'another wife' or 'as the men slaves do' (16a:2-8), no possession (16a:5); THE DEDUCTION AND THE BODY OWNED (Rava 16a:13 — a waiver releases nothing, a document of manumission needed; the price the lower reckoning 20a:9-12, 20b:4-6 'expounded like Sinai'; no partial redemption 20b:8-9); the four ways with severance, three and three (16b:3-8, 16b:17-18); Reish Lakish's father's-death exit refuted (16a:14-16b:10); the two tables of 18a:5 with Rav Sheshet's designated maidservant (18a:6); THE FUGITIVE AND THE SICK (16b:21-17a:4 — sick four years as six; needlework no completion).
- THE PIERCED AND THE JUBILEE: 'forever' UNTIL THE JUBILEE (15a:21-22; 21b:4 — the master's lifetime, the jubilee's forever); the two verses both needed (15a:19-20); THE PIERCED SERVES NO HEIR and the slave serves the son, not the daughter (17b:4-13 — the son stands in his father's place for the designation and the field); THE MAIDSERVANT NEVER PIERCED (15a:3, 15a:5, 15a:18 'man') — 122:9's DISAGREES row against Exodus 21:7 RESOLVED as DATA; 'LIKEWISE' REACHES THE GIFT, NOT THE AWL (17b:10-12 — R. Padda's two conclusions from 'you shall DO').
- THE AWL'S TOOLS AND THE EAR AT THEIR OWN FOLIO (21b:5-22b:5): R. Yosei son of R. Yehuda's amplification-restriction (anything taken in the hand but a corrosive) against Rabbi's GENERAL-PARTICULAR-GENERAL (I6 — metal), 21b:5-10; 'THE awl' the large awl by the article (21b:11); THE RIGHT EAR by 'ear' / 'ear' with the leper (15a:1 — the design's labeled transfer at its Bavli seat); THE UPPER EAR THROUGH THE CARTILAGE because a priest-slave is not pierced lest he be blemished — 'and he shall return to his own family' (21b:7, 21b:12; Bekhorot 37b:12-14 the twin); through the ear until the door (22b:1-2); the door upright and attached (22b:3); the ear's and the door's homilies (22b:4-5).
- THE TWO SAYINGS AND THE RITE'S CONDITIONS: says and repeats, while a slave, at the last peruta (22a:3-6; 15a:4-5); the mutual love and the master's house, illness in either (22a:7-8), both ill an open dilemma (22a:9); THE BOARD 'with you' — not fine bread against coarse, aged wine against new, soft bedding against straw, 'one who acquires a Hebrew slave acquires a master' (20a:14-15, 22a:10); THE NIGHT'S SERVICE the Canaanite maidservant, not labor (15a:11-13 — 123:1's double hire RESOLVED), for a priest-slave Rav permits and Shmuel forbids with Rav Nachman's proof (21b:13-16); THE FAMILY FED (22a:11-13 — 'he and his children with him', 'his wife shall go out with him').
- THE MAIDSERVANT'S SALE, SECOND SALE AND DESIGNATION: not sold twice — 'bevigdo vah' ("dealt deceitfully with her" / "his garment over her") read by the spelling, the vocalization or both (18a:15-18b:2); redeemed against her FATHER's will for the family's flaw (18a:13-14); the designation's form, timing and consent — before two witnesses even at sunset of the last day, R. Yosei son of R. Yehuda's peruta, 'designate her' read 'inform her' (19a:7-19b:2); the stipulation against designation (19b:12-14); the sale to flawed lineage and to relatives (19b:15-20a:5); THE THIEF'S SALE 'for his theft' — the principal, once, R. Eliezer's triumph (18a:8-12); the Jew's slave's relatives an OPEN dilemma (21a:18-19), the gentile's redemption an OBLIGATION (20b:2, 21b:1) in the redeemers' order (21a:16).
- OUTSIDE THE CHAPTER inside the range (58 rows): the walled cities' houses (20b:13-21a:15 — the partial redemption and the relatives), the beautiful captive (21b:17-22a:2 — Deuteronomy 21's), the Canaanite slave's acquisition (22b:6-19 — Mishnah Kiddushin 1:3), the convert's inheritance (17b:14-18a:4), the ladder of Leviticus 25 (20a:17-19; the self-seller's rung 20a:21-22 kept as context).
- THE PLUCKING THAT IS NOT SHEARING (Bekhorot 25a:1-16 — Rav: plucking is not shearing; the cleaver's clearing by hand, not by a tool that looks like shearing, 25a:13; plucked to show a blemish from the outset, 25a:14-16; the red heifer's black-tipped hairs, 25a:8-12) — the ban's definition a DATA row beside 15:19's BLOCK; THE SHED HAIR OF THE BLEMISHED FIRSTLING (Mishnah Bekhorot 3:4 at 25a:17-25b:2 — Akavya ben Mahalalel and the Rabbis; Rava's THREE DISPUTES on where they differ, 26a:6-7; THE RULING AS R. YEHUDA from Eduyot 5:6, 26a:8; the Rabbis' DECREE lest one delay the slaughter to gather wool — the ban's guard, 25b:5, and the offerings that do not atone, 26a:15); the dangling wool defined two ways (26b:5).
- THE TENDING TERM AT ITS BARAITA (Mishnah Bekhorot 4:1 at 26b:8; thirty and fifty from Exodus 22:28-29's juxtaposition, 26b:11-16 — 'THE INTERPRETATION OF THE VERSE WAS GIVEN ONLY TO THE SAGES': the oral number inside the verse, the code/data separation law's seat; the priest who asks within the term looks like a paid helper at the threshing floor, 26b:17).
- 'YEAR BY YEAR' AT ITS OWN FOLIO (27b:3-28a:11): THE FIRSTLING'S OWN YEAR FROM ITS BIRTH (27b:3-6 — 'a year in a year'; 'a lamb in its first year', Leviticus 12:6), TWO DAYS AND A NIGHT ACROSS THE EDGE (27b:5 the school of Rav — as a peace offering; 28a:1 not from 'the breast and the thigh'), THE BLEMISHED GIVEN TO THE PRIEST from 'it shall be yours' and 'their flesh' (28a:3-4 — 'stated nowhere else in the Torah'), THE EXILE'S ARM (28a:6 — no Temple: kept two or three years until a showable blemish), THE THIRTY DAYS after the year for the lost item's return (28a:7-9; R. Elazar's thirty-from-the-blemish CONCLUSIVELY REFUTED, 28a:11 — 28a:10's link row the refuted arm).
- THE BLEMISH SHOWN AFTER THE SLAUGHTER AND THE EXPERT (Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4-5 at 28a:12-28b:7, 28b:17 — R. Yehuda permits, R. Meir forbids AS A PENALTY; the cornea's blemishes change after death, the body's not, 28a:13-28b:4; the non-expert pays a quarter of a small animal or half of a large, 28b:6-7; the paid examiner not trusted unless an expert) — F7's expert beside D1's testimony (35b-37a).
- OUTSIDE THE CHAPTER inside the range (28 rows): the heave-offering from outside the Land (26b:20-27b:2), the erring judge and R. Tarfon's cow (28b:8-16), the lost grave's presumption (25b:12-15).
